<!DOCTYPE html>
<!-- saved from url=(0022)http://localhost:3000/ -->
<html lang="en" class="dark"><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=UTF-8">
  
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>VAULT — Autonomous Distributed Object Storage &amp; Self-Healing Cluster</title>
  
  <!-- Fonts & CDN Icons -->
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/">
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com/" crossorigin="">
  <link href="./VAULT — Autonomous Distributed Object Storage &amp; Self-Healing Cluster_files/css2" rel="stylesheet">
  
  <script src="./VAULT — Autonomous Distributed Object Storage &amp; Self-Healing Cluster_files/saved_resource"></script>
  <script src="./VAULT — Autonomous Distributed Object Storage &amp; Self-Healing Cluster_files/lucide@latest"></script>
  
  <script>
    tailwind.config = {
      darkMode: 'class',
      theme: {
        extend: {
          fontFamily: {
            sans: ['Inter', 'sans-serif'],
            mono: ['"JetBrains Mono"', 'monospace'],
          },
          colors: {
            vault: {
              950: '#030712',
              900: '#070d18',
              850: '#0c1527',
              800: '#111e38',
              700: '#1e293b',
              accent: '#00f2fe',
              emerald: '#10b981',
              rose: '#f43f5e',
              amber: '#f59e0b',
              purple: '#a855f7'
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  </script>

  <style>
    /* Custom Scrollbars */
    ::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 6px; height: 6px; }
    ::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: #030712; }
    ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #1e293b; border-radius: 9999px; }
    ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: #334155; }

    /* Cyber Glass Panels */
    .glass-panel {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(17, 30, 56, 0.75) 0%, rgba(7, 13, 24, 0.9) 100%);
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
      box-shadow: 0 8px 32px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.37);
      backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
    }
    
    .glass-panel-glow {
      position: relative;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(17, 30, 56, 0.75) 0%, rgba(7, 13, 24, 0.9) 100%);
      border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15);
      box-shadow: 0 10px 40px -10px rgba(0, 242, 254, 0.12);
      backdrop-filter: blur(14px);
    }

    /* Ambient Background Grids */
    .bg-grid-cyber {
      background-size: 40px 40px;
      background-image: 
        linear-gradient(to right, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px),
        linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px);
    }

    /* Glow Effects */
    .glow-cyan {
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.35);
    }
    .glow-emerald {
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
    }
    .glow-rose {
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.35);
    }

    /* Radar Pulse Animation */
    @keyframes radarPing {
      0% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 0.9; }
      100% { transform: scale(2.8); opacity: 0; }
    }
    .radar-ping {
      animation: radarPing 1.8s cubic-bezier(0, 0, 0.2, 1) infinite;
    }

    /* Blinking Server LEDs */
    @keyframes blinkLed {
      0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
      50% { opacity: 0.2; }
    }
    .led-blink-fast {
      animation: blinkLed 0.4s infinite;
    }
    .led-blink-normal {
      animation: blinkLed 1s infinite;
    }

    /* Topology Connector Animations */
    @keyframes packetFlow {
      from { stroke-dashoffset: 40; }
      to { stroke-dashoffset: 0; }
    }
    .flowing-packet {
      stroke-dasharray: 6, 8;
      animation: packetFlow 1s linear infinite;
    }

    /* Shimmer Effect for Progress Bars */
    @keyframes shimmer {
      0% { background-position: -200% 0; }
      100% { background-position: 200% 0; }
    }
    .shimmer-bar {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(6,182,212,0.8) 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.4) 50%, rgba(6,182,212,0.8) 100%);
      background-size: 200% 100%;
      animation: shimmer 2s infinite;
    }

    /* Monospace formatting */
    .code-text {
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    }
  </style>

overflow-x-hidden bg-grid-cyber">

  <!-- ========================================================================= -->
  <!-- TOP ALERT BAR: DEMO MODE RUNNER NOTIFICATION -->
  <!-- ========================================================================= -->
  <div id="demoProgressBanner" class="hidden bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 via-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between border-b border-cyan-400/30 sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-lg">
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
      <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white text-cyan-900 font-bold text-[10px] animate-spin">⟳</span>
      <span id="demoStepTitle" class="tracking-wide">AUTOMATED DEMO RUNNING: Step 1/12</span>
      <span id="demoStepDesc" class="text-cyan-100 font-normal hidden md:inline ml-2 opacity-90">— Ingesting high-throughput benchmark payload...</span>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
      <div class="w-32 bg-black/40 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden border border-white/20">
        <div id="demoProgressBar" class="bg-white h-full transition-all duration-300" style="width: 8%"></div>
      </div>
      <button onclick="abortDemo()" class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/40 hover:bg-black/60 text-[11px] text-white">Abort</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col lg:flex-row min-h-screen">

    <!-- ========================================================================= -->
    <!-- SIDEBAR NAVIGATION -->
    <!-- ========================================================================= -->
    <aside class="w-full lg:w-72 bg-vault-900/90 border-r border-slate-800/80 flex flex-col p-5 shrink-0 backdrop-blur-xl z-20">
      
      <!-- Brand & Status -->
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3.5 pb-6 border-b border-slate-800/80">
        <div class="relative flex items-center justify-center w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/20 to-blue-600/10 border border-cyan-500/40 shadow-inner">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="shield-check" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-shield-check text-cyan-400 w-6 h-6"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>
          <span class="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex h-3 w-3">
            <span class="radar-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-cyan-400 opacity-75"></span>
            <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-cyan-500"></span>
          </span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1 class="font-extrabold tracking-wider text-xl text-white">VAULT</h1>
            <span class="text-[10px] bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono">v2.4</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono tracking-tight flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">

            DISTRIBUTED OBJECT FS

      <!-- Navigation Links -->

        <button onclick="switchTab('topology')" id="nav-topology" class="nav-btn w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/40 border-l-4 border-transparent">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="network" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-network w-4 h-4"><rect x="16" y="16" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><rect x="2" y="16" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><rect x="9" y="2" width="6" height="6" rx="1"></rect><path d="M5 16v-3a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h12a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v3"></path><path d="M12 12V8"></path></svg>

          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="copy" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-copy w-4 h-4"><rect width="14" height="14" x="8" y="8" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M4 16c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4c0-1.1.9-2 2-2h10c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2"></path></svg>

          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="wrench" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-wrench w-4 h-4"><path d="M14.7 6.3a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.4l1.6 1.6a1 1 0 0 0 1.4 0l3.106-3.105c.32-.322.863-.22.983.218a6 6 0 0 1-8.259 7.057l-7.91 7.91a1 1 0 0 1-2.999-3l7.91-7.91a6 6 0 0 1 7.057-8.259c.438.12.54.662.219.984z"></path></svg>

        <button onclick="switchTab('rebalancing')" id="nav-rebalancing" class="nav-btn w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/40 border-l-4 border-transparent">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="scale" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-scale w-4 h-4"><path d="M12 3v18"></path><path d="m19 8 3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"></path><path d="M3 7h1a17 17 0 0 0 8-2 17 17 0 0 0 8 2h1"></path><path d="m5 8 3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"></path><path d="M7 21h10"></path></svg>

          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="terminal" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-terminal w-4 h-4"><path d="M12 19h8"></path><path d="m4 17 6-6-6-6"></path></svg>

        <button onclick="switchTab('settings')" id="nav-settings" class="nav-btn w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/40 border-l-4 border-transparent">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="settings" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-settings w-4 h-4"><path d="M9.671 4.136a2.34 2.34 0 0 1 4.659 0 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 3.319 1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1 2.33 4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 0 3.831 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-2.33 4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0-3.319 1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-4.659 0 2.34 2.34 0 0 0-3.32-1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-2.33-4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 0-3.831A2.34 2.34 0 0 1 6.35 6.051a2.34 2.34 0 0 0 3.319-1.915"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"></circle></svg>
          <span>Cluster Settings</span>

      </nav>

      <!-- Cluster Health Monitor Card -->
      <div class="mt-6 glass-panel rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-800/80">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mb-2.5">
          <span class="font-mono uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider text-slate-400">CLUSTER CONSENSUS</span>
          <span id="consensusStatus" class="font-mono text-emerald-400 font-bold">RAFT QUORUM</span>
        </div>
        
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span id="clusterHealthDot" class="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 shadow-emerald-500/50"></span>
            <span id="clusterHealthText" class="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-wide">OPERATIONAL</span>
          </div>
          <span id="durabilityPill" class="text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20">100%</span>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-3.5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800/60 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
          <div>
            <span class="text-slate-400">Replicas:</span>
            <span id="activeReplicaCount" class="text-slate-200 font-bold ml-1">9/9</span>
          </div>
          <div class="text-right">
            <span class="text-slate-400">Heartbeat:</span>
            <span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold ml-1">3.0s</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Quick Audio & Preset Switcher -->
      <div class="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between px-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
        <button onclick="toggleAudio()" id="audioBtn" class="flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="volume-2" aria-hidden="true" id="audioIcon" class="lucide lucide-volume-2 w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400"><path d="M11 4.702a.705.705 0 0 0-1.203-.498L6.413 7.587A1.4 1.4 0 0 1 5.416 8H3a1 1 0 0 0-1 1v6a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h2.416a1.4 1.4 0 0 1 .997.413l3.383 3.384A.705.705 0 0 0 11 19.298z"></path><path d="M16 9a5 5 0 0 1 0 6"></path><path d="M19.364 18.364a9 9 0 0 0 0-12.728"></path></svg>
          <span id="audioLabel">Sound FX: ON</span>
        </button>
        <button onclick="resetClusterState()" class="hover:text-rose-400 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="rotate-ccw" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-rotate-ccw w-3 h-3"><path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 9-9 9.75 9.75 0 0 0-6.74 2.74L3 8"></path><path d="M3 3v5h5"></path></svg>
          <span>Reset Cluster</span>
        </button>
      </div>

    </aside>

    <!-- ========================================================================= -->
    <!-- MAIN APPLICATION WORKSPACE -->
    <!-- ========================================================================= -->
    <main class="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 bg-vault-950/70">
      
      <!-- Top Action Bar -->
      <header class="h-20 border-b border-slate-800/80 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between px-6 lg:px-10 gap-4 bg-vault-900/40 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-30">
        <div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <h2 id="viewHeading" class="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Cluster Health &amp; Telemetry</h2>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-800/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-700/50">LOCAL_SIM://CLUSTER-US-EAST</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Real-time distributed replication, SHA-256 integrity audits &amp; self-healing automation</p>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
          
          <!-- Run Automated Demo Button -->
          <button onclick="triggerAutomatedDemo()" id="runDemoBtn" class="relative group overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 via-teal-400 to-emerald-400 text-slate-950 font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs tracking-wide shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/20 hover:shadow-cyan-400/40 transition-all flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="absolute inset-0 bg-white/20 translate-y-full group-hover:translate-y-0 transition-transform duration-200"></span>
            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="play" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-play w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current"><path d="M5 5a2 2 0 0 1 3.008-1.728l11.997 6.998a2 2 0 0 1 .003 3.458l-12 7A2 2 0 0 1 5 19z"></path></svg>
            <span>▶ Run 12-Step Demo</span>
          </button>

          <!-- Upload Object Button (With Real HTML5 File Reader & Crypto) -->
          <label class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700/90 text-white font-medium px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs border border-slate-700/80 shadow-md transition-all flex items-center gap-2 hover:border-cyan-500/40">
            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="upload-cloud" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-upload-cloud w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400"><path d="M12 13v8"></path><path d="M4 14.899A7 7 0 1 1 15.71 8h1.79a4.5 4.5 0 0 1 2.5 8.242"></path><path d="m8 17 4-4 4 4"></path></svg>
            <span>Upload Object</span>
            <input type="file" id="realFileInput" class="hidden" onchange="handleRealFileUpload(event)">
          </label>

          <!-- Generate Benchmark Payload Button -->
          <button onclick="generateSyntheticObject()" class="bg-slate-800/60 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 font-medium px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs border border-slate-700/60 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-white" title="Generate synthetic test object">
            <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="plus-circle" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-plus-circle w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M8 12h8"></path><path d="M12 8v8"></path></svg>
            <span>+ Synthetic Data</span>
          </button>

        </div>
      </header>

      <!-- Main Body Container -->
      <div class="p-6 lg:p-10 space-y-8 flex-1">

        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <!-- VIEW 1: DASHBOARD -->
        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <section id="view-dashboard" class="space-y-8">
          
          <!-- SIMULATION QUICK ACTIONS BAR -->
          <div class="glass-panel rounded-2xl p-4 border border-cyan-500/20">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="flex h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-pulse"></span>
                <span class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300 font-mono">Failure &amp; Chaos Control Deck</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 hidden md:inline">| Inject anomalies to demonstrate real-time fault tolerance</span>
              </div>
              
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <button onclick="simulateNodeFailure('node-02')" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30 hover:bg-rose-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="zap-off" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-zap-off w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400"><path d="M10.768 5.111 13.44 2.44a1.5 1.5 0 012.474 1.561l-1.633 4.625"></path><path d="m18.889 13.232.672-.672A1.5 1.5 0 0018.5 10h-2.844"></path><path d="m2 2 20 20"></path><path d="m7.94 7.94-3.5 3.499A1.5 1.5 0 005.5 14h4.002a.5.5 0 01.471.666L8.086 20a1.5 1.5 0 002.475 1.56l5.5-5.5"></path></svg>
                  <span>Crash Node-02</span>
                </button>

                <button onclick="triggerAutoRepairCycle()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="heart-pulse" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-heart-pulse w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400"><path d="M2 9.5a5.5 5.5 0 0 1 9.591-3.676.56.56 0 0 0 .818 0A5.49 5.49 0 0 1 22 9.5c0 2.29-1.5 4-3 5.5l-5.492 5.313a2 2 0 0 1-3 .019L5 15c-1.5-1.5-3-3.2-3-5.5"></path><path d="M3.22 13H9.5l.5-1 2 4.5 2-7 1.5 3.5h5.27"></path></svg>
                  <span>Trigger Auto-Heal</span>
                </button>

                <button onclick="injectByteCorruption()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="binary" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-binary w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400"><rect x="14" y="14" width="4" height="6" rx="2"></rect><rect x="6" y="4" width="4" height="6" rx="2"></rect><path d="M6 20h4"></path><path d="M14 10h4"></path><path d="M6 14h2v6"></path><path d="M14 4h2v6"></path></svg>
                  <span>Corrupt Replica (Bit-Rot)</span>
                </button>

                <button onclick="toggleNetworkPartition()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/30 hover:bg-indigo-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="split" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-split w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-400"><path d="M16 3h5v5"></path><path d="M8 3H3v5"></path><path d="M12 22v-8.3a4 4 0 0 0-1.172-2.872L3 3"></path><path d="m15 9 6-6"></path></svg>
                  <span id="btnPartitionLabel">Partition Node</span>
                </button>

                <button onclick="triggerStorageRebalance()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/30 hover:bg-purple-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="scale" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-scale w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400"><path d="M12 3v18"></path><path d="m19 8 3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"></path><path d="M3 7h1a17 17 0 0 0 8-2 17 17 0 0 0 8 2h1"></path><path d="m5 8 3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"></path><path d="M7 21h10"></path></svg>
                  <span>Rebalance Storage</span>
                </button>

                <button onclick="runFullClusterAudit()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30 hover:bg-cyan-500/20 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="shield-alert" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-shield-alert w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="M12 8v4"></path><path d="M12 16h.01"></path></svg>
                  <span>Audit SHA-256</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- TOP 4 STATS METRIC CARDS -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
            
            <!-- Metric 1: Capacity -->
            <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl relative overflow-hidden group hover:border-cyan-500/40 transition-all">
              <div class="absolute -right-3 -top-3 w-20 h-20 bg-cyan-500/5 rounded-full blur-xl group-hover:bg-cyan-500/10 transition-all"></div>
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
                <span class="font-mono uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wider text-slate-400">CLUSTER STORAGE</span>

              </div>

        <section id="view-objects" class="space-y-6 hidden">

        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <!-- VIEW 4: TOPOLOGY & DEEP ARCHITECTURE -->
        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <section id="view-topology" class="space-y-6 hidden">
          
          <div class="pb-2 border-b border-slate-800/80">
            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">System Architecture &amp; Invariant Guarantees</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Formal design specifications, consistency models, and fault-tolerance mechanics</p>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            
            <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-3">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-cyan-400">
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="layers" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-layers w-5 h-5"><path d="M12.83 2.18a2 2 0 0 0-1.66 0L2.6 6.08a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.83l8.58 3.91a2 2 0 0 0 1.66 0l8.58-3.9a1 1 0 0 0 0-1.83z"></path><path d="M2 12a1 1 0 0 0 .58.91l8.6 3.91a2 2 0 0 0 1.65 0l8.58-3.9A1 1 0 0 0 22 12"></path><path d="M2 17a1 1 0 0 0 .58.91l8.6 3.91a2 2 0 0 0 1.65 0l8.58-3.9A1 1 0 0 0 22 17"></path></svg>
              </div>
              <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm">1. Deterministic Placement Engine</h4>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                Utilizes a least-loaded, diversity-enforced placement algorithm. The coordinator guarantees that no two replicas of an object reside on the same physical or virtual storage node.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-3">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400">
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="shield-check" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-shield-check w-5 h-5"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1z"></path><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg>
              </div>
              <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm">2. Continuous Cryptographic Audits</h4>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                Each object replica is audited using <code>crypto.subtle</code> SHA-256 digest hashing. If bit-rot or file tampering is discovered, the replica is instantly cordoned and repaired from a verified peer.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-3">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-purple-400">
                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" data-lucide="git-merge" aria-hidden="true" class="lucide lucide-git-merge w-5 h-5"><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="3"></circle><circle cx="6" cy="6" r="3"></circle><path d="M6 21V9a9 9 0 0 0 9 9"></path></svg>
              </div>
              <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm">3. Automatic Self-Healing Daemon</h4>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                Whenever node heartbeats lapse or replication drops below the configured factor ($R=3$), the self-healing engine locates available healthy nodes and replicates missing byte streams autonomously.
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>

          <!-- Deep Architecture Workflow Visual Diagram -->
          <div class="glass-panel p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800">
            <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm mb-4">Distributed Data Lifecycle Diagram</h4>
            
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3 text-center text-xs">
              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <div class="font-bold text-cyan-400 mb-1">Step 1: Ingestion</div>
                <p class="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">Client streams binary file; gateway calculates SHA-256 checksum.</p>
              </div>

              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <div class="font-bold text-blue-400 mb-1">Step 2: Placement</div>
                <p class="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">Coordinator selects 3 least-utilized healthy nodes (e.g. N1, N3, N5).</p>
              </div>

              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <div class="font-bold text-purple-400 mb-1">Step 3: Replication</div>
                <p class="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">Payload written to 3 isolated node directories simultaneously.</p>
              </div>

              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <div class="font-bold text-amber-400 mb-1">Step 4: Heartbeats</div>
                <p class="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">Nodes ping every 3s. Missing heartbeat flags node as DEGRADED.</p>
              </div>

              <div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <div class="font-bold text-emerald-400 mb-1">Step 5: Auto-Heal</div>

        <!-- VIEW 6: REPLICATION POLICY & PLACEMENT -->
        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <section id="view-replication" class="space-y-6 hidden">
          <div class="pb-2 border-b border-slate-800/80">
            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Replication Policy &amp; Spindle Placement Engine</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Multi-copy redundancy configuration, anti-affinity guarantees, and visual topology trees</p>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-4">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm">Cluster Replication Factor (RF)</h4>
                <span id="rfSliderBadge" class="text-xs font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30">RF = 3</span>
              </div>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Select target redundancy. When set to 3, three identical physical copies of every object are distributed to separate healthy spindles.</p>
              
              <div class="space-y-2 pt-2">
                <div class="flex justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">
                  <span>RF=1 (No Redundancy)</span>
                  <span>RF=5 (Extreme)</span>
                </div>
                <input type="range" id="rfConfigInput" min="1" max="5" value="3" oninput="updateReplicationFactorSetting(this.value)" class="w-full accent-cyan-400 cursor-pointer">
              </div>

              <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 space-y-1">
                <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Anti-Affinity Rule:</span><span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">STRICT (Never co-locate)</span></div>
                <div class="flex justify-between"><span>Minimum Healthy:</span><span class="text-cyan-400 font-bold">2 of 3 Quorum</span></div>
              </div>

        <!-- VIEW 7: SELF-HEALING REPAIRS -->
        <!-- ========================================================================= -->
        <section id="view-repairs" class="space-y-6 hidden">
          <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-2 border-b border-slate-800/80">
            <div>
              <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Self-Healing &amp; Auto-Repair Engine</h3>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Automated replication deficit detection, peer byte streaming, and replacement provisioning</p>

          <div class="pb-2 border-b border-slate-800/80">
            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Cluster Configuration &amp; Durability Policy</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Tune replication thresholds, heartbeat timeouts, and automated repair triggers</p>
          </div>

          <div class="glass-panel p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-6 max-w-2xl">

                <label class="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">Default Replication Factor</label>
                <select id="cfgReplicationFactor" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">
                  <option value="1">1 (Single Copy - No redundancy)</option>
                  <option value="2">2 (Two-way mirror)</option>
                  <option value="3" selected="">3 (Three-way quorum - Recommended)</option>
                  <option value="4">4 (Four-way high availability)</option>
                  <option value="5">5 (Five-way extreme durability)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-1.5">
                <label class="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">Minimum Healthy Replicas</label>
                <input type="number" id="cfgMinHealthy" value="2" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">

              <div class="space-y-1.5">

                <input type="number" id="cfgHeartbeatMs" value="3000" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-1.5">

                <input type="number" id="cfgTimeoutMs" value="6000" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-1.5">
                <label class="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">Rebalancing Imbalance Threshold (%)</label>
                <input type="number" id="cfgRebalancePct" value="20" class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">
              </div>

              <div class="space-y-1.5">

                <select class="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white font-mono">

                  <option>Every 6 Hours</option>
