  <!-- ========================================================================= -->
  <!-- MAIN JAVASCRIPT ENGINE & DISTRIBUTED STATE SIMULATOR -->
  <!-- ========================================================================= -->
  <script>
    /* =========================================================================
       AUDIO SYNTHESIZER (Web Audio API - Sci-Fi UI Feedback)
    ========================================================================= */
    let audioEnabled = true;
    let audioCtx = null;

    function initAudio() {
      if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      }
    }

    function playSound(type) {
      if (!audioEnabled) return;
      try {
        initAudio();
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume();
        }
        
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;

        if (type === 'click') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(800, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(300, now + 0.05);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, now);
          gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, now + 0.05);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.05);
        } else if (type === 'success') {
          osc.type = 'triangle';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, now);
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now + 0.08);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
          gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, now + 0.25);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.25);
        } else if (type === 'error' || type === 'alarm') {
          osc.type = 'sawtooth';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, now);
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, now + 0.1);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
          gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, now + 0.25);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.25);
        } else if (type === 'repair') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(300, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, now + 0.3);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, now);
          gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, now + 0.3);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.3);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Audio might be blocked by browser autoplay policy until first click
      }
    }

    function toggleAudio() {
      audioEnabled = !audioEnabled;
      const icon = document.getElementById('audioIcon');
      const label = document.getElementById('audioLabel');
      if (audioEnabled) {
        icon.classList.remove('text-slate-500');
        icon.classList.add('text-cyan-400');
        label.textContent = "Sound FX: ON";
        playSound('success');
      } else {
        icon.classList.remove('text-cyan-400');
        icon.classList.add('text-slate-500');
        label.textContent = "Sound FX: MUTED";
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       DISTRIBUTED CLUSTER CONFIG & PERSISTENCE MODEL
    ========================================================================= */
    const REPLICATION_FACTOR = 3;
    const CAPACITY_PER_NODE = 100 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024; // 100 GB in bytes
    const STORAGE_DIR_KEY = "vault_cluster_db_v2";

    let nodes = [
      createNode("node-01", "NODE-01 (US-EAST-1A)"),
      createNode("node-02", "NODE-02 (US-EAST-1B)"),
      createNode("node-03", "NODE-03 (US-EAST-1C)"),
      createNode("node-04", "NODE-04 (US-EAST-1D)"),
      createNode("node-05", "NODE-05 (US-EAST-1E)")
    ];

    let objects = [];
    let logs = [];
    let activeRepairs = 0;
    let isDemoRunning = false;
    let demoStepTimer = null;
    let currentModalObjectId = null;

    function createNode(id, hostname) {
      return {
        id,
        hostname,
        status: "HEALTHY", // HEALTHY, DEGRADED, FAILED, RECOVERING
        used: 0,
        capacity: CAPACITY_PER_NODE,
        latencyMs: Math.floor(Math.random() * 12) + 4,
        packetLossRate: 0,
        isPartitioned: false,
        lastHeartbeat: Date.now(),
        cpuPercent: Math.floor(Math.random() * 25) + 10,
        tempC: Math.floor(Math.random() * 8) + 38,
        replicas: [] // List of object IDs assigned
      };
    }

    /* Seed realistic initial data */
    function initializeDefaultSeedData() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_DIR_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          nodes = parsed.nodes || nodes;
          objects = parsed.objects || [];
          logs = parsed.logs || [];
          return;
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("Corrupted local state, regenerating default seeds...");
        }
      }

      // Preload 3 initial realistic objects
      seedDefaultObject(
        "hypervisor-kernel-v5.img",
        542000000, // 542 MB
        "application/x-iso9660-image",
        "4a5e2f7b8c9d0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123",
        ["node-01", "node-02", "node-03"],
        "7f454c4602010100000000000000000003003e00010000007010400000000000"
      );

      seedDefaultObject(
        "satellite-telemetry-oct26.parquet",
        185000000, // 185 MB
        "application/vnd.apache.parquet",
        "9f86d081884c7d659a2feaa0c55ad015a3bf4f1b2b0b822cd15d6c15b0f00a08",
        ["node-02", "node-04", "node-05"],
        "504152311500153c150025022806636f6c756d6e1502192c1500150015020000"
      );

      seedDefaultObject(
        "quantum-encryption-keys.pem",
        3200000, // 3.2 MB
        "application/x-pem-file",
        "2c26b46b68ffc68ff99b453c1d30413413422d706483bfa0f98a5e886266e7ae",
        ["node-01", "node-03", "node-04"],
        "2d2d2d2d2d424547494e2050524956415445204b45592d2d2d2d2d0a4d494945"
      );

      addLog("SUCCESS", "SystemInit", "Vault cluster bootstrap complete. 5 storage nodes registered with Raft consensus.");
      addLog("INFO", "HeartbeatEngine", "Background heartbeat daemon spawned (3000ms loop).");
      addLog("INFO", "IntegrityDaemon", "SHA-256 continuous bit-rot auditor online.");
      saveState();
    }

    function seedDefaultObject(filename, size, mimeType, checksum, nodeIds, hexDump) {
      const objId = "obj-" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8);
      const newObj = {
        id: objId,
        filename,
        size,
        mimeType,
        checksum,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        replicationFactor: REPLICATION_FACTOR,
        status: "HEALTHY",
        hexDump: hexDump || "48656c6c6f20576f726c64205661756c742053746f726167652044617461",
        replicas: []
      };

      nodeIds.forEach(nId => {
        const targetNode = nodes.find(n => n.id === nId);
        if (targetNode) {
          newObj.replicas.push({
            nodeId: nId,
            checksum,
            status: "HEALTHY",
            lastVerified: new Date().toISOString()
          });
          targetNode.used += size;
          targetNode.replicas.push(objId);
        }
      });

      objects.push(newObj);
    }

    function saveState() {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_DIR_KEY, JSON.stringify({
          nodes,
          objects,
          logs: logs.slice(-200)
        }));
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Storage quota limit reached");
      }
    }

    function resetClusterState() {
      if (confirm("Reset cluster to default initial state? All injected failures and uploaded data will be restored.")) {
        localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_DIR_KEY);
        nodes = [
          createNode("node-01", "NODE-01 (US-EAST-1A)"),
          createNode("node-02", "NODE-02 (US-EAST-1B)"),
          createNode("node-03", "NODE-03 (US-EAST-1C)"),
          createNode("node-04", "NODE-04 (US-EAST-1D)"),
          createNode("node-05", "NODE-05 (US-EAST-1E)")
        ];
        objects = [];
        logs = [];
        initializeDefaultSeedData();
        renderAll();
        showToast("Cluster Reset", "Cluster successfully restored to pristine state", "success");
        playSound('success');
      }
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       UTILITY HELPERS (Bytes formatting, Hash, UUID)
    ========================================================================= */
    function formatBytes(bytes) {
      if (bytes === 0) return "0 B";
      const units = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
      const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(1024));
      return `${(bytes / Math.pow(1024, i)).toFixed(i ? 2 : 0)} ${units[i]}`;
    }

    async function computeBufferSha256(arrayBuffer) {
      const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", arrayBuffer);
      const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
      return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    }

    function bufferToHexPreview(uint8Array, maxBytes = 32) {
      return Array.from(uint8Array.slice(0, maxBytes))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
    }

    function addLog(level, source, message, metadata = {}) {
      const logEntry = {
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        level, // INFO, SUCCESS, WARN, ERROR, REPAIR
        source,
        message,
        metadata
      };
      logs.push(logEntry);
      if (logs.length > 250) logs.shift();
      renderLiveTerminalLine(logEntry);
      saveState();
    }

    function sleep(ms) {
      return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       FILE UPLOAD & PLACEMENT LOGIC
    ========================================================================= */
    async function handleRealFileUpload(event) {
      const file = event.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      playSound('click');
      addLog("INFO", "IngressGateway", `Receiving object "${file.name}" (${formatBytes(file.size)}) via HTTP Multipart.`);
      showToast("Ingesting Object", `Streaming ${file.name} to gateway...`, "info");

      // Compute SHA-256 real digest
      const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
      const checksum = await computeBufferSha256(arrayBuffer);
      const hexSample = bufferToHexPreview(new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer), 32);

      // Select 3 least loaded healthy nodes
      const availableNodes = getEligiblePlacementNodes(REPLICATION_FACTOR);
      if (availableNodes.length < REPLICATION_FACTOR) {
        addLog("ERROR", "PlacementEngine", `Insufficient healthy nodes for RF=${REPLICATION_FACTOR}! Found only ${availableNodes.length}.`);
        showToast("Placement Error", "Not enough healthy nodes to satisfy replication factor.", "error");
        playSound('error');
        return;
      }

      const newObject = {
        id: "obj-" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8),
        filename: file.name,
        size: file.size,
        mimeType: file.type || "application/octet-stream",
        checksum,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        replicationFactor: REPLICATION_FACTOR,
        status: "HEALTHY",
        hexDump: hexSample,
        rawBuffer: Array.from(new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer.slice(0, 10000))), // Cache sample for download
        replicas: []
      };

      // Store replicas across target nodes
      availableNodes.forEach(node => {
        newObject.replicas.push({
          nodeId: node.id,
          checksum,
          status: "HEALTHY",
          lastVerified: new Date().toISOString()
        });
        node.used += file.size;
        node.replicas.push(newObject.id);
      });

      objects.unshift(newObject);

      // Trigger packet flow animation on topology diagram
      triggerTopologyIngressAnimation(availableNodes.map(n => n.id));

      addLog("SUCCESS", "ReplicationEngine", `Object ${file.name} replicated 3x across [${availableNodes.map(n => n.id).join(", ")}]. SHA-256 verified.`);
      showToast("Object Uploaded", `${file.name} stored with 3-way redundancy.`, "success");
      playSound('success');

      saveState();
      renderAll();
      event.target.value = "";
    }

    function generateSyntheticObject() {
      playSound('click');
      const randomNames = [
        "cluster-snapshot-2026.tar.gz",
        "financial-ledger-ledger.db",
        "docker-image-vault-worker.tar",
        "genomic-crispr-alignment.fasta",
        "neural-weights-epoch-99.pt"
      ];
      const filename = randomNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * randomNames.length)];
      const size = Math.floor(Math.random() * 450000000) + 20000000; // 20 MB to 470 MB
      
      const dummyBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(filename + Date.now() + "VAULT_SYNTHETIC_DATA_PAYLOAD");
      computeBufferSha256(dummyBytes.buffer).then(checksum => {
        const availableNodes = getEligiblePlacementNodes(REPLICATION_FACTOR);
        if (availableNodes.length < REPLICATION_FACTOR) {
          showToast("Error", "Not enough healthy nodes to place replicas.", "error");
          return;
        }

        const newObj = {
          id: "obj-" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8),
          filename,
          size,
          mimeType: "application/octet-stream",
          checksum,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          replicationFactor: REPLICATION_FACTOR,
          status: "HEALTHY",
          hexDump: "5641554c545f444154415f5041594c4f41445f30783939393939393939393939",
          replicas: []
        };

        availableNodes.forEach(node => {
          newObj.replicas.push({
            nodeId: node.id,
            checksum,
            status: "HEALTHY",
            lastVerified: new Date().toISOString()
          });
          node.used += size;
          node.replicas.push(newObj.id);
        });

        objects.unshift(newObj);
        triggerTopologyIngressAnimation(availableNodes.map(n => n.id));
        addLog("SUCCESS", "PlacementEngine", `Synthetic object "${filename}" generated (${formatBytes(size)}) across ${availableNodes.map(n=>n.id).join(', ')}.`);
        showToast("Synthetic Object Generated", filename, "success");
        saveState();
        renderAll();
      });
    }

    function getEligiblePlacementNodes(count) {
      return nodes
        .filter(n => n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned)
        .sort((a, b) => a.used - b.used)
        .slice(0, count);
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       NODE FAILURE & RECOVERY SIMULATION
    ========================================================================= */
    function simulateNodeFailure(nodeId) {
      const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
      if (!node) return;

      playSound('alarm');
      node.status = "FAILED";
      node.cpuPercent = 0;
      node.tempC = 22;

      addLog("ERROR", "NodeDaemon", `Hardware failure on ${node.hostname} (${node.id})! Node heartbeat dropped.`);

      // Update replicas residing on this node
      let affectedObjects = 0;
      objects.forEach(obj => {
        const replica = obj.replicas.find(r => r.nodeId === node.id);
        if (replica) {
          replica.status = "MISSING";
          obj.status = "DEGRADED";
          affectedObjects++;
        }
      });

      addLog("WARN", "ReplicationMonitor", `Replication deficit detected: ${affectedObjects} objects degraded below RF=${REPLICATION_FACTOR}.`);
      showToast("Node Crash Simulated", `${node.hostname} is now OFFLINE. Cluster degraded.`, "error");

      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    function recoverNode(nodeId) {
      const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
      if (!node) return;

      playSound('click');
      node.status = "RECOVERING";
      addLog("INFO", "NodeDaemon", `${node.hostname} booting up. Running memory checks...`);
      renderAll();

      setTimeout(() => {
        node.status = "HEALTHY";
        node.isPartitioned = false;
        node.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
        node.cpuPercent = Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) + 10;
        node.tempC = 41;

        addLog("SUCCESS", "NodeDaemon", `${node.hostname} reconnected to cluster.`);
        showToast("Node Recovered", `${node.hostname} is back online and accepting reads.`, "success");
        playSound('success');

        // Automatically trigger repair to heal any missing replicas
        triggerAutoRepairCycle();
      }, 1200);
    }

    function recoverAllNodes() {
      nodes.forEach(node => {
        node.status = "HEALTHY";
        node.isPartitioned = false;
        node.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
      });
      addLog("SUCCESS", "ClusterAdmin", "All 5 nodes forcefully restored to HEALTHY.");
      triggerAutoRepairCycle();
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       DATA CORRUPTION & INTEGRITY ENGINE (Bit-Rot Injection)
    ========================================================================= */
    function injectByteCorruption() {
      const eligibleReplicas = [];
      objects.forEach(obj => {
        obj.replicas.forEach(rep => {
          if (rep.status === "HEALTHY") {
            eligibleReplicas.push({ obj, rep });
          }
        });
      });

      if (!eligibleReplicas.length) {
        showToast("No Replicas Available", "Upload objects first to test bit-rot corruption.", "info");
        return;
      }

      playSound('error');
      const target = eligibleReplicas[Math.floor(Math.random() * eligibleReplicas.length)];
      
      // Inject synthetic corrupted hash
      target.rep.checksum = "CORRUPTED_" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 16);
      target.rep.status = "CORRUPTED";
      target.obj.status = "DEGRADED";

      addLog("ERROR", "IntegrityEngine", `Bit-rot corruption detected on ${target.obj.filename} (Node: ${target.rep.nodeId})! SHA-256 hash mismatch.`);
      showToast("Bit-Rot Injected", `Corrupted 1 byte in ${target.obj.filename} on ${target.rep.nodeId}.`, "error");

      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }

    async function runFullClusterAudit() {
      playSound('click');
      addLog("INFO", "IntegrityAudit", "Initiating cryptographic SHA-256 audit across all distributed replicas...");
      showToast("Auditing Cluster", "Computing SHA-256 checksums on all disk blocks...", "info");

      let corruptedCount = 0;
      for (const obj of objects) {
        for (const replica of obj.replicas) {
          if (replica.status === "MISSING") continue;
          if (replica.checksum !== obj.checksum) {
            replica.status = "CORRUPTED";
            obj.status = "DEGRADED";
            corruptedCount++;
          }
        }
      }

      await sleep(600);
      if (corruptedCount > 0) {
        addLog("WARN", "IntegrityAudit", `Audit finished: Discovered ${corruptedCount} corrupted replica blocks! Auto-repair recommended.`);
        showToast("Audit Completed", `Found ${corruptedCount} corrupted blocks. Auto-repair available.`, "error");
        playSound('alarm');
      } else {
        addLog("SUCCESS", "IntegrityAudit", "Audit complete: 100% of stored object replicas verified cryptographically intact.");
        showToast("Integrity Verified", "All SHA-256 digests match. Cluster healthy.", "success");
        playSound('success');
      }

      renderAll();
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       AUTONOMOUS SELF-HEALING & REPAIR ENGINE
    ========================================================================= */
    async function triggerAutoRepairCycle() {
      if (activeRepairs > 0) return;
      activeRepairs = 1;
      document.getElementById('metricActiveRepairCount').textContent = "1";
      playSound('repair');

      addLog("INFO", "SelfHealingDaemon", "Scanning cluster for replication deficits and corrupted replicas...");

      let repairedCount = 0;

      for (const obj of objects) {
        // Find existing healthy copies
        let healthyReplicas = obj.replicas.filter(r => {
          const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === r.nodeId);
          return r.status === "HEALTHY" && node && node.status === "HEALTHY" && !node.isPartitioned;
        });

        // 1. Repair Corrupted Replicas First
        const corruptedIndex = obj.replicas.findIndex(r => r.status === "CORRUPTED");
        if (corruptedIndex !== -1 && healthyReplicas.length > 0) {
          await sleep(500);
          obj.replicas[corruptedIndex].checksum = obj.checksum;
          obj.replicas[corruptedIndex].status = "HEALTHY";
          obj.replicas[corruptedIndex].lastVerified = new Date().toISOString();
          addLog("SUCCESS", "SelfHealingDaemon", `Corrupted replica repaired on ${obj.replicas[corruptedIndex].nodeId} for ${obj.filename}.`);
          repairedCount++;
        }

        // 2. Repair Missing Replicas (due to offline nodes)
        if (healthyReplicas.length < obj.replicationFactor) {
          const needed = obj.replicationFactor - healthyReplicas.length;
          
          for (let i = 0; i < needed; i++) {
            const currentNodesHoldingObj = obj.replicas.map(r => r.nodeId);
            const targetSpareNode = nodes
              .filter(n => n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned && !currentNodesHoldingObj.includes(n.id))
              .sort((a, b) => a.used - b.used)[0];

            if (targetSpareNode) {
              await sleep(600);
              
              // Remove old missing replica and provision new one on spare node
              obj.replicas = obj.replicas.filter(r => r.status !== "MISSING");
              obj.replicas.push({
                nodeId: targetSpareNode.id,
                checksum: obj.checksum,
                status: "HEALTHY",
                lastVerified: new Date().toISOString()
              });

              targetSpareNode.used += obj.size;
              targetSpareNode.replicas.push(obj.id);

              addLog("REPAIR", "SelfHealingDaemon", `Replicated missing block: ${obj.filename} → ${targetSpareNode.hostname}. Redundancy restored.`);
              repairedCount++;
            }
          }
        }

        // Evaluate overall object status
        const validHealthy = obj.replicas.filter(r => {
          const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
          return r.status === "HEALTHY" && n && n.status === "HEALTHY";
        });

        obj.status = (validHealthy.length >= obj.replicationFactor) ? "HEALTHY" : "DEGRADED";
      }

      activeRepairs = 0;
      document.getElementById('metricActiveRepairCount').textContent = "0";

      if (repairedCount > 0) {
        addLog("SUCCESS", "SelfHealingDaemon", `Self-healing completed. Healed ${repairedCount} replicas. Cluster returned to full durability.`);
        showToast("Cluster Repaired", `Autonomous self-healing restored ${repairedCount} replicas.`, "success");
        playSound('success');
      } else {
        addLog("INFO", "SelfHealingDaemon", "Self-healing scan complete. No repairs required.");
      }

      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       NETWORK PARTITION SIMULATION
    ========================================================================= */
    function toggleNetworkPartition() {
      playSound('click');
      const target = nodes.find(n => n.status === "HEALTHY");
      if (!target) return;

      target.isPartitioned = !target.isPartitioned;
      target.status = target.isPartitioned ? "DEGRADED" : "HEALTHY";

      const btn = document.getElementById('btnPartitionLabel');
      if (btn) btn.textContent = target.isPartitioned ? "Heal Partition" : "Partition Node";

      if (target.isPartitioned) {
        addLog("WARN", "NetworkSimulator", `Network split-brain simulated on ${target.hostname}. Quorum heartbeats blocked.`);
        showToast("Network Partition", `${target.hostname} isolated from Raft coordinator.`, "error");
      } else {
        addLog("SUCCESS", "NetworkSimulator", `Network partition resolved for ${target.hostname}. Interconnect restored.`);
        showToast("Partition Healed", `${target.hostname} reconnected to coordinator.`, "success");
      }

      renderAll();
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       BACKGROUND STORAGE REBALANCING
    ========================================================================= */
    async function triggerStorageRebalance() {
      playSound('click');
      addLog("INFO", "Rebalancer", "Calculating storage disparity across active spindles...");
      showToast("Rebalancing", "Evaluating storage distribution across nodes...", "info");

      await sleep(600);
      const healthyNodes = nodes.filter(n => n.status === "HEALTHY");
      if (healthyNodes.length < 2) return;

      const totalUsed = healthyNodes.reduce((acc, n) => acc + n.used, 0);
      const avg = totalUsed / healthyNodes.length;

      addLog("INFO", "Rebalancer", `Average target spindle load: ${formatBytes(avg)}. Analyzing candidate migrations...`);
      await sleep(500);

      addLog("SUCCESS", "Rebalancer", "Cluster storage variance within optimal threshold (σ = 4.2%). No migrations required.");
      showToast("Rebalancing Complete", "Spindle loads are evenly balanced across cluster.", "success");
      playSound('success');
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       THE 12-STEP AUTOMATED HACKATHON DEMO RUNNER
    ========================================================================= */
    const DEMO_STEPS = [
      { title: "Step 1/12: Ingest Benchmark Data", desc: "Generating 250 MB benchmark payload and computing SHA-256 digest..." },
      { title: "Step 2/12: 3-Way Replica Placement", desc: "Coordinator distributing replicas across Node-01, Node-02, and Node-03..." },
      { title: "Step 3/12: Verification", desc: "Verifying initial cluster state: 100% healthy with 3-way redundancy..." },
      { title: "Step 4/12: Hardware Fault Injection", desc: "Simulating hardware power loss on Node-02 (Crash)..." },
      { title: "Step 5/12: Failure Detection", desc: "Heartbeat timeout trips; Coordinator detects replica deficit (3 → 2)..." },
      { title: "Step 6/12: Autonomous Repair", desc: "Self-healing daemon provisions replacement replica on spare Node-04..." },
      { title: "Step 7/12: Redundancy Restored", desc: "Replication factor restored back to 3/3 without administrative intervention..." },
      { title: "Step 8/12: Bit-Rot Tampering", desc: "Injecting silent bit corruption into replica on Node-01..." },
      { title: "Step 9/12: Cryptographic Audit", desc: "Integrity auditor detects SHA-256 hash mismatch on Node-01..." },
      { title: "Step 10/12: Silent Healer Active", desc: "Pulling valid verified byte stream from Node-03 to overwrite corrupted block..." },
      { title: "Step 11/12: Cluster Rebalance", desc: "Running background rebalancing calculations across all active nodes..." },
      { title: "Step 12/12: Full Durability Restored", desc: "Demo Complete: All 12 distributed invariants verified and durable!" }
    ];

    async function triggerAutomatedDemo() {
      if (isDemoRunning) return;
      isDemoRunning = true;
      playSound('click');

      const banner = document.getElementById('demoProgressBanner');
      banner.classList.remove('hidden');

      for (let i = 0; i < DEMO_STEPS.length; i++) {
        if (!isDemoRunning) break;

        const step = DEMO_STEPS[i];
        document.getElementById('demoStepTitle').textContent = `AUTOMATED DEMO: ${step.title}`;
        document.getElementById('demoStepDesc').textContent = `— ${step.desc}`;
        document.getElementById('demoProgressBar').style.width = `${((i + 1) / DEMO_STEPS.length) * 100}%`;

        addLog("INFO", "DemoEngine", `[${step.title}] ${step.desc}`);

        // Execute step actions
        if (i === 0) {
          // Ingest
          generateSyntheticObject();
        } else if (i === 3) {
          // Crash Node-02
          simulateNodeFailure("node-02");
        } else if (i === 5) {
          // Auto heal
          await triggerAutoRepairCycle();
        } else if (i === 7) {
          // Corrupt replica
          injectByteCorruption();
        } else if (i === 8) {
          // Audit
          await runFullClusterAudit();
        } else if (i === 9) {
          // Heal
          await triggerAutoRepairCycle();
        } else if (i === 10) {
          // Rebalance
          await triggerStorageRebalance();
        }

        renderAll();
        await sleep(1800);
      }

      if (isDemoRunning) {
        addLog("SUCCESS", "DemoEngine", "✨ AUTOMATED 12-STEP HACKATHON DEMO COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY!");
        showToast("Demo Completed", "All 12 distributed storage scenarios executed flawlessly.", "success");
        playSound('success');
      }

      abortDemo();
    }

    function abortDemo() {
      isDemoRunning = false;
      document.getElementById('demoProgressBanner').classList.add('hidden');
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       PERIODIC BACKGROUND HEARTBEAT MONITOR (3.0s)
    ========================================================================= */
    setInterval(() => {
      nodes.forEach(node => {
        if (node.status === "FAILED" || node.isPartitioned) return;
        node.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
        // Subtle fluctuation in CPU and Temp for lively simulation
        node.cpuPercent = Math.min(95, Math.max(5, node.cpuPercent + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7) - 3));
      });
      renderMetrics();
    }, 3000);


    /* =========================================================================
       UI RENDERING & CONTROLLERS
    ========================================================================= */
    function switchTab(tabId) {
      playSound('click');
      document.querySelectorAll('section[id^="view-"]').forEach(sec => sec.classList.add('hidden'));
      
      const targetSec = document.getElementById(`view-${tabId}`);
      if (targetSec) targetSec.classList.remove('hidden');

      // Update active nav button styling
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-gradient-to-r', 'from-cyan-500/20', 'to-transparent', 'text-cyan-300', 'border-cyan-400');
        btn.classList.add('text-slate-400', 'border-transparent');
      });

      const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`nav-${tabId}`);
      if (activeBtn) {
        activeBtn.classList.add('bg-gradient-to-r', 'from-cyan-500/20', 'to-transparent', 'text-cyan-300', 'border-cyan-400');
        activeBtn.classList.remove('text-slate-400', 'border-transparent');
      }

      const headings = {
        dashboard: "Cluster Health & Telemetry",
        nodes: "Storage Node Fleet",
        objects: "Distributed Object Explorer",
        topology: "System Architecture & Invariants",
        replication: "Replication Policy & Placement Trees",
        repairs: "Self-Healing & Auto-Repair Engine",
        rebalancing: "Background Storage Rebalancer",
        logs: "Historical Audit Logs",
        settings: "Cluster Configuration & Durability Policy"
      };
      document.getElementById('viewHeading').textContent = headings[tabId] || "Cluster Health";

      renderAll();
    }

    function renderAll() {
      renderMetrics();
      renderDashboardNodeStorage();
      renderFleetGrid();
      renderObjectsTable();
      renderFullLogs();
      renderReplicaTrees();
      renderRepairsTab();
      renderRebalancingTab();
      updateTopologyDiagram();
      if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined' && lucide && lucide.createIcons) {
        try { lucide.createIcons(); } catch (e) {}
      }
    }

    function renderMetrics() {
      const totalUsed = nodes.reduce((acc, n) => acc + n.used, 0);
      const healthyNodes = nodes.filter(n => n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned).length;
      const totalCapacity = CAPACITY_PER_NODE * nodes.length;

      // Calculate durability score
      const totalExpectedReplicas = objects.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.replicationFactor, 0);
      const currentHealthyReplicas = objects.reduce((sum, o) => {
        return sum + o.replicas.filter(r => {
          const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
          return r.status === "HEALTHY" && n && n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned;
        }).length;
      }, 0);

      const durability = totalExpectedReplicas ? Math.round((currentHealthyReplicas / totalExpectedReplicas) * 100) : 100;

      // Update DOM
      document.getElementById('metricTotalUsed').textContent = formatBytes(totalUsed);
      const usedPct = ((totalUsed / totalCapacity) * 100).toFixed(3);
      document.getElementById('metricStoragePercent').textContent = `${usedPct}% Used`;
      document.getElementById('metricStorageBar').style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.max(1, usedPct * 10))}%`;

      document.getElementById('metricHealthyNodes').textContent = `${healthyNodes} / ${nodes.length}`;
      document.getElementById('sidebarNodeBadge').textContent = `${healthyNodes}/${nodes.length}`;
      document.getElementById('metricObjectCount').textContent = objects.length;
      document.getElementById('sidebarObjBadge').textContent = objects.length;
      document.getElementById('activeReplicaCount').textContent = `${currentHealthyReplicas}/${totalExpectedReplicas}`;

      document.getElementById('metricDurabilityScore').textContent = `${durability}%`;
      document.getElementById('durabilityPercentLarge').textContent = `${durability}%`;
      document.getElementById('durabilityPill').textContent = `${durability}%`;

      // Update circular gauge
      const circle = document.getElementById('durabilityCircle');
      if (circle) {
        const offset = 389 - (389 * durability) / 100;
        circle.style.strokeDashoffset = offset;
        circle.style.stroke = durability === 100 ? '#10b981' : (durability > 70 ? '#f59e0b' : '#f43f5e');
      }

      // Update Node status dots in metric card
      const dotsRow = document.getElementById('nodeDotsRow');
      if (dotsRow) {
        dotsRow.innerHTML = nodes.map(n => `
          <span title="${n.id}: ${n.status}" class="w-3 h-3 rounded-full transition-all ${
            n.status === 'HEALTHY' && !n.isPartitioned ? 'bg-emerald-400 glow-emerald' : 
            (n.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bg-rose-500 glow-rose' : 'bg-amber-400')
          }"></span>
        `).join('');
      }

      // Overall cluster operational flag
      const isOperational = (healthyNodes >= 3);
      document.getElementById('clusterHealthDot').className = `w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${isOperational ? 'bg-emerald-500 shadow-emerald-500/50' : 'bg-rose-500 shadow-rose-500/50'}`;
      document.getElementById('clusterHealthText').textContent = isOperational ? "OPERATIONAL" : "QUORUM LOST";
      document.getElementById('consensusStatus').textContent = isOperational ? "RAFT QUORUM" : "DEGRADED";
    }

    function renderDashboardNodeStorage() {
      const container = document.getElementById('dashboardNodeStorageBars');
      if (!container) return;

      container.innerHTML = nodes.map(node => {
        const pct = Math.min(100, (node.used / node.capacity) * 100).toFixed(1);
        const isHealthy = node.status === "HEALTHY" && !node.isPartitioned;

        return `
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono mb-1">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isHealthy ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500'}"></span>
                <span class="text-slate-200 font-semibold">${node.id.toUpperCase()}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal">(${node.hostname})</span>
              </div>
              <div class="text-slate-400">
                <span class="text-white">${formatBytes(node.used)}</span> / ${formatBytes(node.capacity)} (${pct}%)
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
              <div class="h-full transition-all duration-300 ${isHealthy ? 'bg-cyan-400' : 'bg-rose-500'}" style="width: ${Math.max(2, pct)}%"></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderFleetGrid() {
      const grid = document.getElementById('nodeFleetCardsGrid');
      if (!grid) return;

      grid.innerHTML = nodes.map(node => {
        const isHealthy = node.status === "HEALTHY" && !node.isPartitioned;
        const pct = Math.min(100, (node.used / node.capacity) * 100).toFixed(1);
        const lastBeatSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - node.lastHeartbeat) / 1000);

        return `
          <div class="glass-panel p-5 rounded-2xl border transition-all ${isHealthy ? 'border-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-500/40' : 'border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-950/20'}">
            
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-800/80">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div class="relative w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <i data-lucide="server" class="w-5 h-5 ${isHealthy ? 'text-cyan-400' : 'text-rose-400'}"></i>
                  <span class="absolute top-1 right-1 w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isHealthy ? 'bg-emerald-400 led-blink-normal' : 'bg-rose-500'}"></span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 class="font-bold text-white text-sm font-mono">${node.hostname}</h4>
                  <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">DIR: ./storage/${node.id}/</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <span class="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                node.status === 'HEALTHY' && !node.isPartitioned ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30' : 
                (node.status === 'FAILED' ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30' : 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30')
              }">
                ${node.isPartitioned ? 'PARTITIONED' : node.status}
              </span>
            </div>

            <!-- Specs Grid -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 my-4 text-[11px] font-mono">
              <div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800">
                <span class="text-slate-400 text-[10px]">LATENCY</span>
                <div class="text-slate-200 font-bold mt-0.5">${node.latencyMs} ms</div>
              </div>
              <div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800">
                <span class="text-slate-400 text-[10px]">CPU / TEMP</span>
                <div class="text-slate-200 font-bold mt-0.5">${node.cpuPercent}% / ${node.tempC}°C</div>
              </div>
              <div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800">
                <span class="text-slate-400 text-[10px]">REPLICAS</span>
                <div class="text-cyan-400 font-bold mt-0.5">${node.replicas.length} objects</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Capacity progress -->
            <div class="space-y-1 mb-4">
              <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                <span>Disk Usage:</span>
                <span class="text-white">${formatBytes(node.used)} (${pct}%)</span>
              </div>
              <div class="w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
                <div class="bg-cyan-400 h-full" style="width: ${Math.max(2, pct)}%"></div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono mb-4">
              <span>Last Heartbeat: ${lastBeatSec}s ago</span>
              <span class="text-emerald-400">IOPS: 1,420 r/w</span>
            </div>

            <!-- Node Simulation Actions -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
              ${node.status !== 'FAILED' ? `
                <button onclick="simulateNodeFailure('${node.id}')" class="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/10 hover:bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30 text-xs font-mono transition-colors">
                  Crash Node
                </button>
              ` : `
                <button onclick="recoverNode('${node.id}')" class="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/10 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 text-xs font-mono transition-colors">
                  Recover Node
                </button>
              `}
              
              <button onclick="toggleNodeLatency('${node.id}')" class="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-mono transition-colors">
                ${node.latencyMs > 100 ? 'Normal Latency' : 'Throttle (500ms)'}
              </button>
            </div>

          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function toggleNodeLatency(nodeId) {
      playSound('click');
      const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
      if (!node) return;
      node.latencyMs = (node.latencyMs > 100) ? 8 : 500;
      addLog("WARN", "NetworkSimulator", `Artificially set latency on ${node.hostname} to ${node.latencyMs}ms.`);
      renderAll();
    }

    function renderObjectsTable() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('objectsTableBody');
      if (!tbody) return;

      const query = (document.getElementById('objectSearchQuery')?.value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
      const filtered = objects.filter(obj => 
        obj.filename.toLowerCase().includes(query) || 
        obj.checksum.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        obj.id.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      );

      if (!filtered.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `
          <tr>
            <td colspan="6" class="py-10 text-center text-slate-500 font-sans">
              No distributed objects found matching your query.
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
        return;
      }

      tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(obj => {
        const healthyReplicas = obj.replicas.filter(r => {
          const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
          return r.status === "HEALTHY" && n && n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned;
        });

        const isFullyDurable = healthyReplicas.length >= obj.replicationFactor;

        return `
          <tr class="hover:bg-slate-900/60 transition-colors">
            
            <td class="py-3 px-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <i data-lucide="file" class="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400 shrink-0"></i>
                <div>
                  <div class="font-bold text-white hover:underline cursor-pointer" onclick="openObjectDetailModal('${obj.id}')">${escapeHtml(obj.filename)}</div>
                  <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">${obj.id}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </td>

            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-300">
              ${formatBytes(obj.size)}
            </td>

            <td class="py-3 px-4">
              <span class="text-cyan-300 text-[11px] bg-cyan-950/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-800/40 select-all">
                ${obj.checksum.slice(0, 14)}...${obj.checksum.slice(-6)}
              </span>
            </td>

            <td class="py-3 px-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                ${obj.replicas.map(r => {
                  const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
                  const isNodeOk = n && n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned;
                  const isOk = r.status === "HEALTHY" && isNodeOk;
                  return `
                    <span class="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                      isOk ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20' : 
                      (r.status === 'CORRUPTED' ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40' : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40')
                    }">
                      ${r.nodeId.replace('node-', 'N')}
                    </span>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </div>
            </td>

            <td class="py-3 px-4">
              <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                isFullyDurable ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30' : 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30'
              }">
                ${healthyReplicas.length}/${obj.replicationFactor} REPLICAS
              </span>
            </td>

            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <button onclick="openObjectDetailModal('${obj.id}')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-cyan-300 transition-colors" title="Inspect Details">
                  <i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                </button>
                <button onclick="downloadObjectById('${obj.id}')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 transition-colors" title="Download Verified File">
                  <i data-lucide="download" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                </button>
                <button onclick="deleteObjectById('${obj.id}')" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-950 text-rose-400 transition-colors" title="Delete Object">
                  <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                </button>
              </div>
            </td>

          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderLiveTerminalLine(log) {
      const container = document.getElementById('liveTerminalLogs');
      if (!container) return;

      const line = document.createElement('div');
      line.className = "flex items-start gap-2 py-0.5 border-b border-slate-900/60";

      const timeStr = new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString();
      const levelColors = {
        INFO: "text-cyan-400",
        SUCCESS: "text-emerald-400",
        WARN: "text-amber-400",
        ERROR: "text-rose-400 font-bold",
        REPAIR: "text-purple-400 font-bold"
      };

      line.innerHTML = `
        <span class="text-slate-600 shrink-0">${timeStr}</span>
        <span class="${levelColors[log.level] || 'text-slate-400'} shrink-0">[${log.level}]</span>
        <span class="text-purple-300 shrink-0">&lt;${log.source}&gt;</span>
        <span class="text-slate-300">${escapeHtml(log.message)}</span>
      `;

      container.appendChild(line);
      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
    }

    function renderFullLogs() {
      const container = document.getElementById('fullAuditLogsContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      const query = (document.getElementById('logFilterQuery')?.value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
      const filtered = logs.slice().reverse().filter(l => 
        l.message.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        l.source.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        l.level.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      );

      container.innerHTML = filtered.map(log => {
        const timeStr = new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString();
        return `
          <div class="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-3">
            <span class="text-slate-500 shrink-0">${timeStr}</span>
            <span class="font-bold shrink-0 ${log.level === 'ERROR' ? 'text-rose-400' : (log.level === 'SUCCESS' ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-cyan-400')}">[${log.level}]</span>
            <span class="text-purple-300 shrink-0">&lt;${log.source}&gt;</span>
            <span class="text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(log.message)}</span>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function clearTerminalLogs() {
      logs = [];
      const term = document.getElementById('liveTerminalLogs');
      if (term) term.innerHTML = "";
      renderFullLogs();
      addLog("INFO", "Terminal", "Audit log buffer cleared.");
    }

    function exportLogsJson() {
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(logs, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `vault-audit-log-${Date.now()}.json`;
      a.click();
    } 

    /* =========================================================================
       REPLICATION & TOPOLOGY TREES RENDERER
    ========================================================================= */
    function renderReplicaTrees() {
      const container = document.getElementById('replicaTreesContainer');
      if (!container) return;
      if (!objects.length) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div class="p-6 text-center text-slate-500">No objects distributed across spindles yet.</div>`;
        return;
      }
      container.innerHTML = objects.map(obj => {
        const treeLines = obj.replicas.map((rep, idx) => {
          const isLast = idx === obj.replicas.length - 1;
          const prefix = isLast ? '└── ' : '├── ';
          const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === rep.nodeId);
          const isOk = rep.status === 'HEALTHY' && node && node.status === 'HEALTHY' && !node.isPartitioned;
          return `
            <div class="pl-4 py-0.5 text-xs ${isOk ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-rose-400 font-bold'}">
              <span>${prefix}</span>
              <span class="text-white">${node ? node.hostname : rep.nodeId}</span>
              <span class="text-[10px] ml-1.5 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded ${isOk ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300'}">[${rep.status}]</span>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');

        return `
          <div class="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800">
            <div class="font-bold text-cyan-300 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>📁</span>
                <span>${escapeHtml(obj.filename)}</span>
              </span>
              <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">${formatBytes(obj.size)} | SHA: ${obj.checksum.slice(0, 10)}...</span>
            </div>
            <div class="mt-2 space-y-0.5">${treeLines}</div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderRepairsTab() {
      const tbody = document.getElementById('repairDeficitTableBody');
      if (!tbody) return;
      let deficitCount = 0;
      tbody.innerHTML = objects.map(obj => {
        const healthyCount = obj.replicas.filter(r => {
          const n = nodes.find(x => x.id === r.nodeId);
          return r.status === 'HEALTHY' && n && n.status === 'HEALTHY' && !n.isPartitioned;
        }).length;
        const deficit = Math.max(0, obj.replicationFactor - healthyCount);
        if (deficit > 0) deficitCount++;
        return `
          <tr class="hover:bg-slate-900/50">
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(obj.filename)}</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-cyan-300">${obj.replicationFactor} Copies</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-200">${healthyCount} Online</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3 ${deficit > 0 ? 'text-rose-400 font-bold' : 'text-emerald-400'}">${deficit > 0 ? `-${deficit} Missing` : 'None (Full Quorum)'}</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3">
              <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${deficit > 0 ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400'}">
                ${deficit > 0 ? 'DEFICIT (DEGRADED)' : 'OPTIMAL'}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">
              <button onclick="triggerAutoRepairCycle()" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 text-xs">Heal Now</button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');

      const defEl = document.getElementById('repairDeficitStat');
      if (defEl) defEl.textContent = deficitCount;
      const actEl = document.getElementById('repairActiveStat');
      if (actEl) actEl.textContent = activeRepairs;
    }

    function renderRebalancingTab() {
      const list = document.getElementById('rebalanceSpindlesList');
      if (!list) return;
      const avg = nodes.reduce((a, b) => a + b.used, 0) / nodes.length;
      list.innerHTML = nodes.map(n => {
        const pct = Math.min(100, (n.used / n.capacity) * 100).toFixed(1);
        const isOver = n.used > avg * 1.2 && n.used > 0;
        return `
          <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 space-y-1.5 font-mono text-xs">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${n.status === 'HEALTHY' && !n.isPartitioned ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500'}"></span>
                <span class="font-bold text-white">${n.hostname}</span>
              </div>
              <span class="${isOver ? 'text-amber-400 font-bold' : 'text-slate-400'}">${formatBytes(n.used)} (${pct}%) ${isOver ? '⚠ HOT SPINDLE' : 'BALANCED'}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full bg-slate-950 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
              <div class="h-full ${isOver ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-cyan-400'}" style="width: ${Math.max(2, pct)}%"></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function updateReplicationFactorSetting(val) {
      const badge = document.getElementById('rfSliderBadge');
      if (badge) badge.textContent = `RF = ${val}`;
      addLog("INFO", "PolicyEngine", `Replication factor dynamically tuned to ${val}.`);
    }

    function saveClusterPolicySettings() {
      playSound('success');
      const note = document.getElementById('policySavedNote');
      if (note) {
        note.classList.remove('hidden');
        setTimeout(() => note.classList.add('hidden'), 3000);
      }
      showToast("Policy Saved", "Durability policies successfully persisted to cluster.", "success");
      addLog("SUCCESS", "Admin", "Cluster durability policy updated and applied.");
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       DYNAMIC TOPOLOGY ANIMATION TRIGGER
    ========================================================================= */
    function updateTopologyDiagram() {
      nodes.forEach(node => {
        const badge = document.getElementById(`topo-badge-${node.id}`);
        const path = document.getElementById(`path-${node.id}`);
        if (!badge) return;

        const isHealthy = node.status === "HEALTHY" && !node.isPartitioned;
        badge.innerHTML = `
          <span class="text-slate-200 font-semibold">${node.id.toUpperCase()}</span>
          <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${isHealthy ? 'bg-emerald-400 glow-emerald' : 'bg-rose-500 glow-rose'}"></span>
        `;

        if (path) {
          path.setAttribute('stroke', isHealthy ? '#38bdf8' : '#e11d48');
        }
      });
    }

    function triggerTopologyIngressAnimation(targetNodeIds) {
      targetNodeIds.forEach(id => {
        const path = document.getElementById(`path-${id}`);
        if (path) {
          path.setAttribute('stroke-width', '4');
          path.classList.add('flowing-packet');
          setTimeout(() => {
            path.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');
          }, 2000);
        }
      });
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       OBJECT DETAIL MODAL & BYTE INSPECTOR
    ========================================================================= */
    function openObjectDetailModal(objectId) {
      playSound('click');
      const obj = objects.find(o => o.id === objectId);
      if (!obj) return;
      currentModalObjectId = objectId;

      document.getElementById('modalObjName').textContent = obj.filename;
      document.getElementById('modalObjId').textContent = obj.id;
      document.getElementById('modalObjSize').textContent = formatBytes(obj.size);
      document.getElementById('modalObjMime').textContent = obj.mimeType;
      document.getElementById('modalObjRf').textContent = `${obj.replicas.length}/${obj.replicationFactor} Copies`;
      document.getElementById('modalObjStatus').textContent = obj.status;
      document.getElementById('modalObjStatus').className = `font-bold mt-1 ${obj.status === 'HEALTHY' ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`;
      document.getElementById('modalObjHash').textContent = obj.checksum;

      // Render replicas container
      const repContainer = document.getElementById('modalReplicasContainer');
      repContainer.innerHTML = obj.replicas.map(r => {
        const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
        const isOk = r.status === "HEALTHY" && n && n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned;

        return `
          <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-xs">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${isOk ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500'}"></span>
              <div>
                <span class="text-white font-bold">${n ? n.hostname : r.nodeId}</span>
                <span class="text-slate-500 text-[10px] ml-2">(${r.nodeId})</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span class="text-[11px] text-cyan-300">${r.checksum.slice(0, 16)}...</span>
              <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${isOk ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400'}">
                ${r.status}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');

      // Render Hex Dump preview
      const hasCorruptedReplica = obj.replicas.some(r => r.status === "CORRUPTED");
      const hexEl = document.getElementById('modalHexDump');
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('modalByteStatus');

      if (hasCorruptedReplica) {
        statusEl.textContent = "ERR_CHECKSUM_MISMATCH: BIT-ROT DETECTED";
        statusEl.className = "text-rose-400 font-bold text-[11px]";
        hexEl.innerHTML = `
          <div class="text-rose-400">00000000: 7f 45 4c 46 <span class="bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold px-1 rounded">DE AD BE EF</span> 00 00 00 00 03 00 3e 00 .ELF....>..</div>
          <div class="text-slate-400">00000010: 70 10 40 00 00 00 00 00 40 00 00 00 00 00 00 00 p.@.....@.......</div>
        `;
      } else {
        statusEl.textContent = "ALL BYTES VERIFIED (SHA-256 MATCH)";
        statusEl.className = "text-emerald-400 text-[11px]";
        hexEl.innerHTML = `
          <div>00000000: 7f 45 4c 46 02 01 01 00 00 00 00 00 03 00 3e 00 .ELF..........>.</div>
          <div>00000010: 70 10 40 00 00 00 00 00 40 00 00 00 00 00 00 00 p.@.....@.......</div>
        `;
      }

      document.getElementById('objectDetailModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeObjectModal() {
      playSound('click');
      document.getElementById('objectDetailModal').classList.add('hidden');
      currentModalObjectId = null;
    }

    function corruptModalObject() {
      if (!currentModalObjectId) return;
      const obj = objects.find(o => o.id === currentModalObjectId);
      if (!obj) return;
      
      const rep = obj.replicas.find(r => r.status === "HEALTHY");
      if (rep) {
        rep.status = "CORRUPTED";
        rep.checksum = "CORRUPTED_" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 10);
        obj.status = "DEGRADED";
        playSound('error');
        addLog("ERROR", "IntegrityEngine", `Injected corruption into replica of ${obj.filename} on ${rep.nodeId}.`);
        openObjectDetailModal(currentModalObjectId);
        renderAll();
      }
    }

    function repairModalObject() {
      if (!currentModalObjectId) return;
      triggerAutoRepairCycle().then(() => {
        openObjectDetailModal(currentModalObjectId);
      });
    }

    function downloadModalObject() {
      if (!currentModalObjectId) return;
      downloadObjectById(currentModalObjectId);
    }

    function downloadObjectById(objectId) {
      playSound('click');
      const obj = objects.find(o => o.id === objectId);
      if (!obj) return;

      // Find first verified healthy replica
      const healthyRep = obj.replicas.find(r => {
        const n = nodes.find(node => node.id === r.nodeId);
        return r.status === "HEALTHY" && n && n.status === "HEALTHY" && !n.isPartitioned;
      });

      if (!healthyRep) {
        showToast("Download Blocked", "No healthy replicas currently online to serve this object.", "error");
        playSound('error');
        return;
      }

      addLog("INFO", "ObjectRetrieval", `Serving ${obj.filename} from healthy spindle ${healthyRep.nodeId}. Cryptographic check PASSED.`);
      
      // Trigger actual browser download
      const content = `VAULT_OBJECT_STORAGE_DATA\nFilename: ${obj.filename}\nUUID: ${obj.id}\nSHA-256: ${obj.checksum}\nNode-Origin: ${healthyRep.nodeId}\nTimestamp: ${new Date().toISOString()}`;
      const blob = new Blob([content], { type: obj.mimeType });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = obj.filename;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      showToast("Download Started", `${obj.filename} verified & served from ${healthyRep.nodeId}.`, "success");
      playSound('success');
    }

    function deleteObjectById(objectId) {
      if (!confirm("Delete object and purge all replicas across cluster?")) return;
      playSound('click');
      const index = objects.findIndex(o => o.id === objectId);
      if (index === -1) return;

      const obj = objects[index];
      // Deduct used space on nodes
      obj.replicas.forEach(r => {
        const node = nodes.find(n => n.id === r.nodeId);
        if (node) {
          node.used = Math.max(0, node.used - obj.size);
          node.replicas = node.replicas.filter(id => id !== obj.id);
        }
      });

      objects.splice(index, 1);
      addLog("INFO", "ObjectManager", `Purged object ${obj.filename} (${obj.id}) from cluster.`);
      showToast("Object Purged", `${obj.filename} deleted from all nodes.`, "info");
      saveState();
      renderAll();
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       TOAST NOTIFICATION ENGINE
    ========================================================================= */
    function showToast(title, message, type = "info") {
      const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
      if (!container) return;

      const toast = document.createElement('div');
      toast.className = `p-4 rounded-xl border backdrop-blur-xl shadow-xl flex items-start gap-3 pointer-events-auto transform transition-all duration-300 translate-y-2 opacity-0 max-w-sm ${
        type === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-950/80 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-200' :
        (type === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-950/80 border-rose-500/40 text-rose-200' : 'bg-slate-900/90 border-cyan-500/40 text-slate-200')
      }`;

      const icon = type === 'success' ? '✓' : (type === 'error' ? '⚠' : 'ℹ');

      toast.innerHTML = `
        <span class="text-base font-bold shrink-0">${icon}</span>
        <div>
          <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] opacity-90 mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(message)}</div>
        </div>
      `;

      container.appendChild(toast);

      // Animate in
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove('translate-y-2', 'opacity-0');
      }, 10);

      // Auto dismiss
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'translate-x-4');
        setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
      }, 4000);
    }

    function escapeHtml(str) {
      return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
        .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
        .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
        .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
        .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
    }


    /* =========================================================================
       LIVE BACKEND REST API SYNCHRONIZER
    ========================================================================= */
    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://localhost:3000';
    let backendConnected = false;

    async function checkBackendConnection() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/health`, { cache: 'no-store' });
        if (res.ok) {
          backendConnected = true;
          const data = await res.json();
          const cons = document.getElementById('consensusStatus');
          if (cons) cons.textContent = data.consensus || "RAFT QUORUM";
          await syncFromBackend();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        backendConnected = false;
      }
    }

    async function syncFromBackend() {
      if (!backendConnected) return;
      try {
        const [nodesRes, objsRes, logsRes] = await Promise.all([
          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/nodes`),
          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/objects`),
          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/logs`)
        ]);
        if (nodesRes.ok) {
          const bNodes = await nodesRes.json();
          bNodes.forEach(bn => {
            const loc = nodes.find(n => n.id === bn.id);
            if (loc) {
              loc.status = bn.status;
              loc.used = bn.used;
              loc.isPartitioned = bn.is_partitioned;
            }
          });
        }
        if (objsRes.ok) {
          const bObjs = await objsRes.json();
          if (Array.isArray(bObjs) && bObjs.length > 0) {
            objects = bObjs.map(bo => ({
              id: bo.id,
              filename: bo.filename,
              size: bo.size,
              mimeType: bo.mime_type,
              checksum: bo.checksum,
              createdAt: bo.created_at,
              replicationFactor: bo.replication_factor,
              status: bo.status,
              hexDump: "7f 45 4c 46 02 01 01 00 00 00 00 00",
              replicas: bo.replicas.map(r => ({
                nodeId: r.node_id,
                checksum: r.checksum,
                status: r.status,
                lastVerified: r.last_verified
              }))
            }));
          }
        }
        renderAll();
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    /* =========================================================================
       INITIAL LAUNCH SEQUENCE
    ========================================================================= */
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      initializeDefaultSeedData();
      renderAll();
      checkBackendConnection();
      setInterval(checkBackendConnection, 3000);
    });
  </script>



</body></html>
